HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_03896532b2e40bcb6407","text":"3. **Obscured sight becomes a condition for harmful perception.** Fixed **غ س ق B003** supplies an eye losing brightness, and fixed **و ق ب B001** includes the eye socket among its recesses. This reverses 113:1 **ف ل ق B002**, clarification after obscurity, and is sharpened by 113:5 **ح س د B001**, whose scope begins with seeing another's blessing and wishing its removal. The packet supplies darkened sight and hostile seeing; I infer that perceptual failure can be ethical as well as optical, allowing a seen good to be recoded as a target. The fixed ayah can thus be read as refuge from a mode of vision darkened in what it makes of what it sees.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_126843c7ec18a828f469","text":"2. **A flow enters a receptive hollow and can be retained there.** Fixed **غ س ق B004** supplies flowing or pouring, including night pouring over terrain, while fixed **و ق ب B001** supplies a recess that receives something. In 113:4, **ن ف ث B001** supplies emitted moisture or venom, **ن ف ث B002** emitted blood, **ع ق د B001** fastening, and **ع ق د B003** liquid becoming firm. The packet supplies flow, cavity, emission, and fixation; I infer a transport chain in which a subtle medium becomes harmful when it enters a receiving site and is held there. *غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ* therefore gains a material reading of seepage or infiltration alongside nocturnal entry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B003","root_001670/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1a502f16139a955632cb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:3:3\",\"root_001086\",[\"B003\"]],[\"113:3:5\",\"root_001670\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gözün kararması, başkasındaki iyiliği hedefe dönüştüren bozulmuş bir görme ve değerlendirme biçimini de düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001670/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1a8608ec342e7d6dbdd3","text":"4. **Household vulnerability under cover.** Evidence: `و ق ب B005` household effects and `و ق ب B001` apertures; activated by `ع ق د B015` waist-knot nearness, `ع ق د B016` enclosure around a place, and `ع و ذ B004` clinging close beside something. Mechanism: hollows and household goods turn darkness into an invasion of intimate spaces. I infer that the danger of night is its access to what is enclosed, close, and domestic. Reading change: the ayah protects not only a traveler outside but the interior space where hidden harm can enter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2e0c8111ab9843810f7d","text":"113:4 changed the fixed pair most strongly: **ن ف ث B001** and **ن ف ث B002**, together with **ع ق د B001** and **ع ق د B003**, turned flowing **غ س ق B004** plus receptive **و ق ب B001** into a delivery, entry, and retention sequence. 113:5 added a different surprise: because **ح س د B001** begins with seeing another's blessing, fixed **غ س ق B003** (the eye darkening) acquired an ethical-perceptual relation, not just an optical one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001670/B001","root_001670/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_37230e45285a16e372e3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:3:5\",\"root_001670\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Karanlığın karanlığın kendisinden çok onun içeri girip alanı gizliliğe bürüdüğü eşikte yoğunlaşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_46a8d61d2b40c6fd282c","text":"1. **Darkness as descent into concealment.** Evidence: `غ س ق B001` night darkness settling in and `و ق ب B002` entering into concealment as darkness descends; activated by `ف ل ق B002` dawn splitting open. Mechanism: the supplied branches form a reversible threshold: dawn opens; night enters and hides. I infer that the harm lies not simply in night as time but in the moment when concealment takes possession of the field. Reading change: `إِذَا وَقَبَ` becomes the critical event of entry/covering, not a redundant time marker.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B004","root_001670/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_72a6ea14f40dfc020dd1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:3:3\",\"root_001086\",[\"B004\"]],[\"113:3:5\",\"root_001670\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karanlık, bir akışın alıcı bir oyuğa sızıp orada tutulması gibi maddi bir giriş ve yerleşme boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B003","root_001670/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8ca56848d55198b9dd52","text":"113:4 and 113:5 changed this ayah away from a merely nocturnal scene. The following knot-and-breath ayah showed that hidden harm can be inserted into media, which made `و ق ب B001/B002` feel like any receptive aperture, not just the arrival of night. The final envier then activated `غ س ق B003`: darkness can be a darkening of sight and evaluation. The surprise is that night became both atmosphere and model: concealment is the condition under which breath, knots, and envy can operate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9a58fa203b1e0679604f","text":"1. **The dangerous moment is ingress, not darkness treated as a static substance.** Fixed **غ س ق B001** supplies night darkness settling in, and fixed **و ق ب B002** supplies entry into concealment; the conditional *إِذَا* joins the actor-state to the event. Earlier 113:1 **ف ل ق B002** supplies dawn splitting open and disclosure after obscurity. The packet supplies opposed transitions, disclosure and concealed entry; I infer that concealment enables otherwise detectable harms. The fixed ayah now concentrates on the threshold at which obscurity penetrates a place or situation, rather than condemning every state of darkness indiscriminately.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_af4577a848602b23804c","text":"2. **Night as substance filling hollows.** Evidence: `غ س ق B004` flowing or pouring out and `و ق ب B001` a recess or hollow that receives something; activated by `خ ل ق B011` water-holding hollow, `ع ق د B003` thickening/hardening, and `ن ف ث B002` a wound sending blood out. Mechanism: the packet supplies several images of liquid or semi-liquid movement into containers and out of wounds. I infer that darkness can be read materially, as something poured into receptive recesses. Reading change: the ayah becomes tactile and spatial: harm seeps, fills, and occupies cavities rather than merely arriving as a clock-time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001086/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_eb52ebf0476f0fc44166","text":"3. **Darkened seeing and envy.** Evidence: `غ س ق B003` the eye becoming darkened; activated by `ح س د B001` seeing another's blessing and wanting its removal, plus `ع و ذ B002` protection from fear, illness, or evil eye. Mechanism: the packet supplies eye-darkening and the envier's visual relation to another's blessing. I infer that the descending darkness also names a failure of sight: perception itself becomes occluded or hostile. Reading change: `غَاسِقٍ` can read as optical/affective darkness, not only the cosmic night.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
